As function h_i:
- if i < n-s: h_i appears as second: term - h_{i+s} h_i -> slope -h_{i+s}
- if i ≥ s: h_i appears as first: term h_i - h_i h_{i-s} -> slope 1 - h_{i-s}

Q_s = ∑ h_i - h_i h_{i+s}
- if i < n-s: first: slope 1 - h_{i+s}
- if i ≥ s: second: slope -h_{i-s}

slope_i(Q_s) = (1-h_{i+s} if i<n-s else 0) + (-h_{i-s} if i>=s else 0).

Variable_update algorithm details:
```
def variable_update(h, p, q, idx, lam, tol=1e-10, maxbin=35):
    n = len(h)
    old = h[idx]
    sp = np.empty(n); sq = np.empty(n)
    sp[0] = 1.0 - 2.0*old
    sq[0] = sp[0]
    if n>1:
       sp[1:]=0.0; sq[1:]=0.0
       r = n - idx - 1
       if r>0:
          right = h[idx+1:idx+1+r]
          sp[1:r+1] -= right
          sq[1:r+1] += 1.0 - right
       lf = idx
       if lf>0:
          left = h[idx-1:-1:-1]  # length idx; if idx=0 empty; if idx= n? okay.
          sp[1:lf+1] += 1.0 - left
          sq[1:lf+1] -= left
    # binary for affine min
    lo=0.0; hi=1.0; x0=old
    for it in range(maxbin):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       d=mid-old
       v0 = p[0] + (1.0-2.0*old)*d
       # compute pval/qval max and active
       if n>1:
          vp = p[1:] + sp[1:]*d
          vmax_p = vp.max(); smax_p = (sp[1:] + lam)? no derivative later
          vq = q[1:] + sq[1:]*d
          vmax_q = vq.max()
       maxv = v0
       if vmax_p > maxv: maxv=vmax_p
       if vmax_q > maxv: maxv=vmax_q
       # active slopes with lam
       smin = np.inf; smax = -np.inf
       act_tol = 1e-9*max(1.0, abs(maxv))
       if v0 >= maxv - act_tol:
          s0 = 1.0 - 2.0*old + lam
          if s0 < smin: smin=s0
          if s0 > smax: smax=s0
       if n>1:
          mask = vp >= maxv - act_tol
          if np.any(mask):
             slopes = sp[1:][mask] + lam
             ...
          maskq = vq >= maxv - act_tol
       if smin+lam? Wait I already added lam to s0? For other if not. Let's add lam after collecting term slopes to avoid double.
```

Coordinate descent loop:
```
def coordinate_descent(h, p, q, n, max_sweeps=20, deadline=None, lam=0.0, lam_rate=0.05, order='sequential'/'random'):
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       idxs = np.arange(n); if random shuffle
       for i in idxs:
          improved = variable_update(...)
          if improved:
             # update lam based on sum
             s = h.sum()
             if abs(s-target)>1e-12:
                lam += lam_rate*(s-target)
                lam = clip(lam, -2,2)
             if abs(s-target)>1e-6:
                # maybe every 50 updates project
       if sweep % 5 ==0: project_feasible; recompute p,q; maybe reset lam? 
       check time
       if objective no change for sweeps break
```

Build Sp/Sq efficiently:
```
def build_slopes(h):
    n=len(h); Sp=np.empty((n,n)); Sq=np.empty((n,n))
    for i in range(n):
       Sp[i,0]=1-2h[i]; Sq[i,0]=Sp[i,0]
       if i>0: Sp[i,1:i+1] += 1-h[0:i][::-1]? Need fill segments.
```
Can build via loops over s maybe easier O(n^2): for s in 1..n-1:
   For i=0..n-s-1 (right neighbor exists): Sp[i,s] += -h[i+s]; Sq[i,s] += 1-h[i+s]
   For i=s..n-1 (left neighbor exists): Sp[i,s] += 1-h[i-s]; Sq[i,s] += -h[i-s]
Initialize zeros. Complexity O(n^2). For n=500, 250k loops vectorized by s:
```
Sp=np.zeros((n,n)); Sq=np.zeros((n,n)); diag=1-2h; Sp[np.arange(n),0]=diag; Sq...
for s in range(1,n):
    r = n-s
    Sp[:r,s] -= h[s:]
    Sq[:r,s] += 1-h[s:]
    Sp[s:,s] += 1-h[:r]
    Sq[s:,s] -= h[:r]
```
Yes vectorized per s. Good.

Similarly pair_update with rows:
```
def pair_update_matrix(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,...):
   oldi=h[i], oldj=h[j]
   dmin = max(-oldi, oldj-1); dmax = min(1-oldi, oldj)
   rowp_i=Sp[i]; rowp_j=Sp[j]; diffp = rowp_i - rowp_j
   diffq = Sq[i] - Sq[j]
   row0_slope = rowp_i[0] - rowp_j[0]  # (1-2oldi) - (1-2oldj) = 2(oldj-oldi)
   # binary over d in [dmin,dmax], affine f: max(p0 + row0_slope*d, p[1:]+diffp[1:]*d, q[1:]+diffq[1:]*d)
   eval derivative with slopes diffp/diffq and row0_slope.
   candidates: d0, dmin,dmax,0
   actual: d0 exact: p0_new = p[0] + (newi(1-newi)-oldi(1-oldi)) + (newj(1-newj)-oldj(1-oldj)); max(p0_new, p[1:]+diffp[1:]*d.max, q...)
   accept if < current max - tol.
   update p/q and slopes for i +d, j -d (call update_slopes twice) and h.
```
This allows exact mass-preserving local search. Great.

Slope update function:
```
def update_slopes(Sp,Sq,k,d):
    n=Sp.shape[0]
    if d==0: return
    Sp[k,0] -= 2*d; Sq[k,0] -= 2*d
    r=n-k-1
    if r>0:
        s_idx = np.arange(1,r+1)
        rows = np.arange(k+1,k+1+r)
        Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d; Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d
    lf=k
    if lf>0:
        s_idx = np.arange(1,lf+1)
        rows = k - s_idx
        Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d; Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d
```

  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try parent=initial_h_values.astype(float); n_parent=len(parent); sample c
  except: parent=None
  best_h=None; best_c=inf

def coordinate_descend(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n,rng,deadline, max_sweeps=None, lam=0.0, no_improve_limit=3):
    target=n/2
    sweeps=0
    no_imp=0
    best_omax = np.max(np.maximum(p,q))
    while time.time()<deadline and sweeps<max_sweeps:
       order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
       sweep_improved=False
       for idx in order:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          # skip if variable near bound? If old <1e-8 or >1-1e-8, could still move? If at bound, line search may try inward; useful for bounds? yes allow.
          old_omax=... maybe use current p/q max
          new_omax, lam, improved = variable_update(...)
          if improved:
             s=h.sum()
             lam += 0.1*(s-target); clip(-5,5)
             if abs(s-target)>0.5? maybe project? Could not.
       omax=np.max(np.maximum(p,q))
       if omax < best_omax - 1e-10:
           best_omax=omax; no_imp=0; sweep_improved=True
       else:
           no_imp+=1
       # recompute to remove drift and maybe adjust lam to 0? 
       h=project_feasible(h,n) # every sweep? Could be costly O(n) only; then recompute p/q/slopes O(n^2)
       p,q=recompute; Sp,Sq=build_slopes(h)
       # lam maybe attenuate after projection? set lam=0? If projection exact, mass okay.
       lam = 0.0? Maybe keep small: lam *=0.5
       sweeps+=1
       if no_improve_limit and no_imp>=2: break
    return h,p,q,Sp,Sq,lam
```

Pseudo for binary inside min_max_affine_delta:
```
def find_delta_affine(base0, arr1, arr2, slope0, sarr1, sarr2, lam, dlo, dhi, maxit=25):
    if dhi <= dlo: return 0.5*(dlo+dhi)
    lo=dlo; hi=dhi
    for _ in range(maxit):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       v0=base0+slope0*mid
       if arr1.size:
          v1=arr1+sarr1*mid; m1=v1.max()
       else: m1=-inf
       if arr2.size:
          v2=arr2+sarr2*mid; m2=v2.max()
       else: m2=-inf
       maxv=max(v0,m1,m2)
       tol=1e-8*max(1.0,abs(maxv))
       smin=1e100; smax=-1e100
       if v0 >= maxv - tol:
          s=slope0
          if s<smin:smin=s
          if s>smax:smax=s
       if arr1.size:
          # avoid allocating v1 again? reuse v1 if created.
          mask = v1 >= maxv - tol
          if np.any(mask):
             sl = sarr1[mask]
             smin = min(smin, sl.min()); smax=max(smax, sl.max())
       arr2 similar
       if smax == -1e100: # fallback: use direction of slope0? 
          mid? 
       if smax + lam < -1e-9:
          lo=mid
       elif smin + lam > 1e-9:
          hi=mid
       else:
          break
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```

```
def eval_var_actual(p,q,rowp,rowq,old,delta):
    new=old+delta
    if new<0 or >1: return inf
    p0 = p[0] + new*(1-new) - old*(1-old)
    val = p0
    if len(p)>1:
       v1max = np.max(p[1:]+rowp[1:]*delta)
       if v1max>val: val=v1max
       v2max = np.max(q[1:]+rowq[1:]*delta)
       if v2max>val: val=v2max
    return val
```

def pair_eval(p1,q1,p0,oldi,oldj,diffp,diffq,d,sdist):
   newi=oldi+d; newj=oldj-d
   if newi<0 or newi>1 or newj<0 or newj>1: return inf
   p0v = p0 + newi*(1-newi)-oldi*(1-oldi)+newj*(1-newj)-oldj*(1-oldj)

      vmax=vp.max()

         cv=vp[sdist-1]+d*d
         if cv>vmax: vmax=cv
      if vmax>val: val=vmax
      vq=q1+diffq*d
      vmax2=vq.max()
      if sdist>0...
   return val
```

def apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,d,sdist):

   newi=oldi+d; newj=oldj-d
   if p[0]: p[0]+=...

```

Need be careful when updating slopes for i and j: slope matrices entries depend on both variables; applying sequentially with old values? Suppose entry affected by both changes? For pair i,j, some slope entries might depend on both h_i and h_j? Row slopes for variable r, col s depends on neighbors h[r±s], not on h_r except s=0. If r equals one of i,j, and neighbor equals the other, then that slope entry depends on the other variable. Sequential updating: update slopes for i using old h_j? Then j change will also update same entry if it is neighbor. Need total change is -δ_i -δ_j for that entry? Let's see: Slope entry Sp[t,s] decreases by total change of its neighbor variable(s) (it depends on one or two neighbors). If changing i and j, an entry might depend on both if i and j are the two neighbors of t for same s? For Sp[t,s] has at most two contributions: -h[t+s] and 1-h[t-s]. If i=t+s and j=t-s, both endpoints. Then value changes by -di -dj? Wait contributions: -h[i] + 1 - h[j]. If h_i changes d, h_j changes -d, total change to slope entry = -d -(-? h_j entry coefficient -1, so 1-h_j change = -(-d)=+d). They cancel! Let's derive: h_i +d, h_j -d: -h_i -> -d, 1-h_j -> +d, net 0. Sequential updates: update_slopes(i,d) will decrement entry by d if i neighbor; update_slopes(j,-d) will decrement entry by -d? Our update_slopes subtracts d for a variable change δ: entry -= δ. For j δ=-d, entry -= (-d) = +d. Net 0. Good. Sequential works. For s=0, each only own. For p/q update cross? p_s for term involving i,j: linear diff plus d^2; slope updates cancel, consistent.

Build/recompute functions:
```
def recompute_pq(h):
    n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h, mode='full')
    p=c[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
    q=c[:n][::-1].copy()
    p[0]=np.sum(h*(1-h)) # central should be same; enforce
    q[0]=p[0]
    return p,q
```

Project feasible:
Need preserve step values and sum. Implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff=target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          # distribute proportional to room
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # zero out tiny
    h[(h<1e-15)] = 0; h[(h>1-1e-15)] =1?
    # exact fix
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       idx=np.flatnonzero((h>1e-10)&(h<1-1e-10))
       if idx.size:
          h[idx] += diff/idx.size
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          # fallback distribute over all
          h += diff/n
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If n odd target .5, projection okay. If diff after clip maybe small. Sum may not be exact machine but okay.
Could also use Wasserstein? Fine.

def random_smooth_h(n, seed):

def active_lags(p,q,tol=1e-8):
    om=max(max(p),max(q))
    tol=1e-8*max(1,om)
    act_p=[]; act_q=[]
    if p[0]>=om-tol: act_p.append(0)
    for s in 1..n-1:
       if p[s]>=om-tol: act_p.append(s)
       if q[s]>=om-tol: act_q.append(s)
    return om, act_p, act_q

Resampling method:
```
def resample_h(h, new_n):
    old_n=len(h)
    x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)*2.0/old_n
    if new_n == old_n: return h.copy()
    x_new=(np.arange(new_n)+0.5)*2.0/new_n
    # linear interpolation for centers
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, new_n)
```
For downsample, linear interpolation may not conserve exactly but projection. Could also average blocks: If old_n >= new_n, use `np.repeat`? I think interp okay. Step function coarse not exact but initial.

`compute_active(p,q,om,tol)`:
Return `act_p` list of s where p[s] >= om-tol, `act_q`, and `p0_active` bool.
```
def active_lists(p,q,om,tol=1e-8):
    qt=1e-8*max(1.0,om)
    act_p=[]; act_q=[]
    if p[0] >= om-qt:
       act_p.append(0)
    for s in range(1,len(p)):
       if p[s] >= om-qt: act_p.append(s)
    # q0 skip? If q0 active, same as p0; maybe include in act_q? For pair p0 active considered separately. If include q0 too, slope same; okay but duplicate. We'll include only p0 in act_p; if q0 active but p0 not? They equal, so no.
    for s in range(1,len(p)):
       if q[s] >= om-qt: act_q.append(s)
    p0_active = (len(p)>0 and p[0] >= om-qt)
    return act_p, act_q, p0_active
```
For pair possible, need slopes for active p_s/q_s including p0? For p0, slope = Sp[i,0]-Sp[j,0] = 1-2oldi - (1-2oldj)=2(oldj-oldi). But p0 is concave; we use p0_active separately. For affine active excluding p0, loops act_p without 0 and act_q.
In `all_pairs`, if p0_active, allow extremity directions. Then also check affine active for other directions? We can compute smax/smin for act_p/act_q (excluding 0).
If p0_active and variables unequal, maybe only extremity direction first; but affine may also allow opposite. We'll allow both if p0_active and either inequality or active. Good.

Direction definitions:
- We parameterize d = h_i + d, h_j - d.
- p0 extremity: if h_i > h_j, increasing h_i and decreasing h_j (d positive) makes more extreme. So p0_active allows + if h_i > h_j + eps. If h_j > h_i, - if d negative. If nearly equal, both.
- Affine active: directional derivative for +d is max active slopes (excluding p0? include if p0 active maybe but concave; skip for direction because linear check may be conservative). If max_slope < -tol, +d lowers affine part. If min_slope > tol, -d lowers affine part.
Potential sign: For variable pair, slope diff for lag l = derivative of F_l with respect to d. If smax<0, moving +d decreases all active terms -> max decreases initially. Yes.

Let's implement `optimize_candidate` strategy per n:
```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,alloc,deadline,rng):
    t_start=time.time()
    stop = min(deadline, t_start+alloc)
    h=project_feasible(h,n); p,q=recompute; Sp,Sq=build
    best_c = max(max(p),max(q)) * 2/n
    best_h = h.copy()
    # If n <= 350:
       all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, stop - 2, delta_sets, max_passes maybe until no)
    else:
       random_pair_search(... stop-2, delta_sets, attempts based)
    # recompute best
    c=...
    if c<best_c: best...
    # Perturbation loop with remaining:
    while time < stop-5:
       scale = ...
       hp=best_h.copy(); hp+=rng.normal; project
       p,q=...
       if n<=350: all_pairs_search with smaller list and time min(30, remaining*0.25)
       else: random_search min...
       update best
    final project, c
    return best_h,c,n
```

Pair i,j distance s. In pair_eval, for d, p1 array corresponds p_s for s=1..n-1 with index s-1. We add cross d^2 at index sdist-1. But is it always p_s and q_s? Suppose i<j. p_shift s includes pair (i,j) as right j, left i. q_shift s includes pair left i, right j. Both have cross +d^2 with d = h_i +d, h_j -d? Derivation: q term h_i(1-h_j), α=d (i), β=-d (j) => -αβ=+d^2. yes. Good.
What about if i>j? We always set i<j in loops. d parameter still h_i +d, h_j -d; distance s = j-i. Pair term p_s: right j, left i? Since j>i, h_j(1-h_i), α_j=-d? Wait d parameter: h_i +=d, h_j -=d (because j is "j" variable with -d). For p_s term with right h_j (change -d) and left h_i (change d). Δ = -d - [(-d)*d? let's use formula u=h_j (α=-d), v=h_i (β=d): α - αβ = -d - (-d*d)= -d +d^2. Linear diff Sp[i]-Sp[j]: Sp[i,s] for left = -h_j? Wait i left, j right. Sp[i,s] = -h_j (only) because i is left index? For p_s derivative wrt left h_i = -h_j. Sp[j,s] = 1-h_i. diff = -h_j - (1-h_i)=hi-hj-1. Linear change = d(hi-hj-1). Cross +d^2. Exact: h_j - h_j h_i: (bj-d)(1-(bi+d)) - bj(1-bi) = -d - (bj bi + bj d - bi d - d^2)+bj bi = -d -bj d +bi d +d^2 = d(bi-bj-1)+d^2. Matches. Good. For q_s term h_i(1-h_j): (bi+d)(1-(bj-d)) - ... = d + bi d -bj d +d^2 = d(1+bi-bj)+d^2. diff Sq[i]-Sq[j]: Sq[i] left=1-hj; Sq[j] right=-hi; diff=1-hj+hi =1+hi-hj. ok.
Thus cross add independent.

So within a pass, p/q remain exact without recompute (modulo floating). Nice.

Can we use this exact update for variable coordinate? For one variable, p_s linear; p0 exact. Yes.

Could we do a global pair descent with line search exact using F(d) = max of affine + two +d^2 + one -2d^2? Let's see if we can solve exactly with candidates. F(d) is max of:
- A0(d) = p0 + a0 d - 2d^2 (concave)
- For s=dist: p_s + a_s d + d^2, q_s + b_s d + d^2 (convex)
- Other affines.
This max of one concave, two convex, affines. It is generally not convex due concave. But since concave term A0 is bounded above by line; the minimum might at endpoints or stationary of a convex part. We can find candidate d by active line search and evaluate endpoints + line intersections perhaps. Fixed deltas easier.

Could do ternary on a regularization? no.

Fixed deltas down to 1e-5 should suffice for local. To achieve c5 improvement 1e-5, h delta 1e-4 maybe enough. Smallest delta 1e-5 okay.

Let's include in delta list: 1e-4,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5,5e-6,1e-6. But all_pairs with 1e-6 for n=250 maybe 31k extra eval, okay. We can avoid 1e-6 for many candidates; only final best.

Let's write functions top-level.

`compute_c5(h):`
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return inf
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
Maybe if h has tiny negative due rounding? project ensures.

`project_feasible(h,n):` As above. Need avoid modifying input. Use float array. At end, maybe set tiny values to 0 or 1? If we clamp, sum changes. Better only if abs(h)<1e-15? That may alter sum by negligible. Could skip. Ensure no values outside due clip.
Potential issue: iterative projection with room if diff>0 and all h=1? target<=n, if all 1 and target<n, diff negative, subtract. Fine.

`resample_h(h,new_n):` Use linear interpolation then project. For n small, if parent has high frequencies, interpolation okay.
```
def resample_h(old_h, new_n):
    old_n=len(old_h)
    if old_n == new_n: return old_h.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)*(2.0/old_n)
    x_new=(np.arange(new_n)+0.5)*(2.0/new_n)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,old_h), new_n)
```
If old_n < new_n, interp upsamples. Good.

`random_fourier_h(n, seed):`
```
def random_fourier_h(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    # use random number of modes 2-4
    for k in range(1,6):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.05,0.5) * (0.55**k)
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       h += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + ph)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Maybe use mode 1? If mode 1 sine mean zero. Good. Could also start binary block:
```
def random_block_h(n, seed):

    pos=0

       length = max(1,int(n/rng?))

       h[pos:pos+length]=val

```
Maybe not needed.

`recompute_pq(h):`
```
def recompute_pq(h):
    n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    p=c[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
    q=c[:n][::-1].copy()
    p0=float(np.dot(h,1.0-h))
    p[0]=p0; q[0]=p0
    return p,q
```
`np.dot` exact sum. c central may differ by float; set.

`build_slopes(h):`
```
def build_slopes(h):
    n=len(h)
    Sp=np.zeros((n,n),dtype=float)
    Sq=np.zeros((n,n),dtype=float)
    idx=np.arange(n)
    Sp[idx,0]=1.0-2.0*h
    Sq[idx,0]=1.0-2.0*h
    for s in range(1,n):
       r=n-s
       # if r==0 skip (but s<n so r>=1)
       Sp[:r, s] -= h[s:]
       Sq[:r, s] += 1.0 - h[s:]
       Sp[s:, s] += 1.0 - h[:r]
       Sq[s:, s] -= h[:r]
    return Sp,Sq
```
Check second assignment `Sp[s:,s]` length r; `1.0 - h[:r]` right. Good.
Memory for n=800: 640k*2? 800x800 float 5.1MB each, okay. n=1600 20MB each, maybe okay but we avoid.

`update_slopes(Sp,Sq,k,d):`
```
def update_slopes(Sp,Sq,k,d):
    if d==0.0: return
    n=Sp.shape[0]
    Sp[k,0] -= 2.0*d
    Sq[k,0] -= 2.0*d
    lf=k
    if lf>0:
       s_idx=np.arange(1,lf+1)
       rows=k - s_idx
       Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d
       Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d
    r=n-k-1
    if r>0:
       s_idx=np.arange(1,r+1)
       rows=k + s_idx
       Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d
       Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d
```
Need when k=n-1, r=0. Good.
But earlier we reasoned all neighbor entries decrement by δ. Let's verify with a case: Sp[i,s] = 1-h[left] - h[right]. If h[k] (neighbor) changes δ, value decrement δ. Yes. Squ? same. Good.

`pair_eval(p0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, d, sdist):`
Need p1/q1 views length n-1. If n=1, no. For n>1.
```
def pair_eval(p0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, d, sdist):
    newi=oldi+d
    newj=oldj-d
    if newi < -1e-15 or newi > 1.0+1e-15 or newj < -1e-15 or newj > 1.0+1e-15:
       return float('inf')
    # clip for p0? Use exact if within tolerance; if slight out, set to 0/1?
    if newi < 0.0: newi=0.0
    elif newi > 1.0: newi=1.0
    if newj < 0.0: newj=0.0
    elif newj > 1.0: newj=1.0
    p0v = p0 + newi*(1.0-newi) - oldi*(1.0-oldi) + newj*(1.0-newj) - oldj*(1.0-oldj)
    val=p0v
    if p1.size:
       # use local variables for speed
       vp = p1 + dp1*d
       v = vp.max()
       if sdist > 0 and sdist <= p1.size:
          cv = vp[sdist-1] + d*d
          if cv > v: v = cv
       if v > val: val = v
       vq = q1 + dq1*d
       v = vq.max()
       if sdist > 0 and sdist <= p1.size:
          cv = vq[sdist-1] + d*d
          if cv > v: v = cv
       if v > val: val = v
    return val
```
Could optimize by computing cross with if d positive/negative. d*d.
Potential numerical if d=-0 and sdist index? okay.

`apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,d,sdist):`
```
def apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,d,sdist):
    if d==0: return h,p,q,Sp,Sq
    n=len(h)
    oldi=h[i]; oldj=h[j]
    newi=oldi+d; newj=oldj-d
    # clip
    if newi<0: newi=0.0
    elif newi>1: newi=1.0
    if newj<0: newj=0.0
    elif newj>1: newj=1.0
    p[0] = p[0] + newi*(1-newi)-oldi*(1-oldi)+newj*(1-newj)-oldj*(1-oldj)
    if n>1:
       # Use current Sp rows before update
       p[1:] += (Sp[i]-Sp[j])[1:] * d
       q[1:] += (Sq[i]-Sq[j])[1:] * d
       if sdist>0:
          p[sdist] += d*d
          q[sdist] += d*d
    h[i]=newi; h[j]=newj
    update_slopes(Sp,Sq,i,d)
    update_slopes(Sp,Sq,j,-d)
    return h,p,q,Sp,Sq
```
But computing `Sp[i]-Sp[j]` inside apply allocates; all_pairs already has dp/dq. We can pass dp1,dq1 to avoid recompute. Modify signature: `apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,d,sdist,dp1,dq1)`. Then use p[1:] += dp1*d, q[1:] += dq1*d. But dp1 view computed from old Sp/Sq. Good.
```

   ... if n>1: p[1:] += dp1*d; q[1:] += dq1*d; cross...
```
Need p/q update using old dp1. In all_pairs, we compute dp/dq before evaluate. If we choose best_d from multiple d candidates, dp unchanged until apply. Good.
But if `p[1:] += dp1*d` modifies p; dp1 view into Sp not p. Good.

In all_pairs, after accept, recomputing active may require exact p/q. But p/q increment with dp1 based on old Sp. If previous p/q had rounding, okay.

`active_data(p,q,om,tol)`:
```
def active_data(p,q,om,tol):
    act_p=[]; act_q=[]
    if p[0] >= om - tol: act_p.append(0) # but for slopes exclude maybe; keep
    for s in range(1,len(p)):
       if p[s] >= om - tol: act_p.append(s)
    for s in range(1,len(p)):
       if q[s] >= om - tol: act_q.append(s)
    p0_active = p[0] >= om - tol
    return act_p, act_q, p0_active
```
To avoid including q0, q[0] same; no.

act_p_nonzero = [s for s in act_p if s !=0]
act_q_nonzero = act_q
```
Update possible:
```
def pair_directions(h,Sp,Sq,i,j,act_p,act_q,p0_active,oldi,oldj,tol_slope):
    allow_pos=False; allow_neg=False
    if p0_active:
       if oldi > oldj + 1e-10: allow_pos=True
       elif oldj > oldi + 1e-10: allow_neg=True
       else: allow_pos=allow_neg=True
    if act_p or act_q:
       smin=1e100; smax=-1e100
       rowpi=Sp[i]; rowpj=Sp[j]
       for s in act_p:
          if s==0: continue
          g=rowpi[s]-rowpj[s]
          if g<smin: smin=g
          if g>smax: smax=g
       rowqi=Sq[i]; rowqj=Sq[j]
       for s in act_q:
          g=rowqi[s]-rowqj[s]
          update
       if smax < -tol_slope: allow_pos=True
       if smin > tol_slope: allow_neg=True
    return allow_pos,allow_neg
```
If act_p only p0 active and no q0, smin inf; no affine.

Slope tol: maybe `1e-8 + 1e-7*max(1,om)`? For slopes units O(1), use `1e-8` or `1e-7`. Since values ~50, floating slopes ~1, tolerance 1e-9 enough. Use `slope_tol=1e-8`.

All_pairs_search code with recompute:
```
def all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n,deadline,deltas,max_recompute_updates=25):
    if n <2: return h,p,q,Sp,Sq
    p1=p[1:]; q1=q[1:] # But p[1:] view; after recompute need refresh.
    total_passes = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
       act_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7*om)
       act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p,q,om,act_tol)
       delta_set = deltas
       improved=0
       recompute_count=0
       for i in range(n-1):
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           oldi = h[i]
           for j in range(i+1,n):
               if time.time() > deadline: break
               oldj=h[j]
               # directions
               allow_pos=False; allow_neg=False
               if p0_active:
                   if oldi > oldj + 1e-10: allow_pos=True
                   elif oldj > oldi + 1e-10: allow_neg=True
                   else: allow_pos=allow_neg=True
               # If active arrays moderate, compute
               if act_p or act_q:
                   smin=1e100; smax=-1e100
                   rowpi=Sp[i]; rowpj=Sp[j]
                   for s in act_p:
                       if s==0: continue
                       g=rowpi[s]-rowpj[s]
                       if g<smin: smin=g
                       if g>smax: smax=g
                   rowqi=Sq[i]; rowqj=Sq[j]
                   for s in act_q:
                       g=rowqi[s]-rowqj[s]
                       if g<smin: smin=g
                       if g>smax: smax=g
                   if smax < -1e-8: allow_pos=True
                   if smin > 1e-8: allow_neg=True
               if not (allow_pos or allow_neg): continue
               dmax_pos = min(1.0-oldi, oldj)
               dmax_neg = min(oldi, 1.0-oldj)
               if dmax_pos <= 0 and dmax_neg <= 0: continue
               # For speed, if active arrays very large, directions maybe already allow; okay
               sdist = j-i
               # compute diff rows
               dp = Sp[i] - Sp[j]
               dq = Sq[i] - Sq[j]
               dp1 = dp[1:]; dq1 = dq[1:]
               oldp0 = p[0]; oldi_val=oldi; oldj_val=oldj
               best_d=0.0; best_val=om
               if allow_pos and dmax_pos > 0:
                   for d in delta_set:
                       if d > dmax_pos + 1e-12: continue
                       val=pair_eval(oldp0,p1,q1,oldi_val,oldj_val,dp1,dq1,d,sdist)
                       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_d=d
               if allow_neg and dmax_neg > 0:
                   nmag = dmax_neg
                   for d in delta_set:
                       if d > nmag + 1e-12: continue
                       val=pair_eval(oldp0,p1,q1,oldi_val,oldj_val,dp1,dq1,-d,sdist)
                       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_d=-d
               if best_d != 0.0 and best_val < om - 1e-9:
                   # apply
                   apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,best_d,sdist,dp1,dq1)
                   om = best_val
                   improved +=1
                   # update oldi for subsequent j
                   oldi = h[i]
                   recompute_count +=1
                   if recompute_count >= 25:
                       p,q = recompute_pq(h)
                       Sp,Sq = build_slopes(h)
                       p1=p[1:]; q1=q[1:]
                       om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
                       act_tol=max(1e-12,1e-7*om)
                       act_p,act_q,p0_active=active_data(p,q,om,act_tol)
                       recompute_count=0
                       oldi = h[i]
       if improved ==0:
           break
       # end pass: recompute to be safe
       p,q = recompute_pq(h)
       Sp,Sq = build_slopes(h)
       # maybe if time break
    return h,p,q,Sp,Sq
```

- `om` and `best_val`: If p0 active/extremity, best_val from candidate. After many accepts, om variable may be less than true max due drift/active stale. If recompute not yet, p[1:] updates exact, so best_val true if previous p exact. Good.
- Improve tolerance: use `improve_tol = 1e-9` constant? Better `1e-11*max(1,om)`? Let's set `improve_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-10*om)` (~5e-9 count). In code: `if best_val < om - max(1e-12, 1e-10*om):`. This stops after small.
- Active tol for directions maybe `max(1e-9, 1e-6*om)`? We used 1e-7*om for active list? Need choose. If active tol 1e-7*om ~5e-6 count, includes near. Good. If active list grows, fine.

def random_pair_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n,deadline,deltas,attempts_max=None):

Now, `optimize_candidate` detailed:
```
def optimize_candidate(h_start,n,time_alloc,deadline,rng):
    t0=time.time(); stop=min(deadline, t0+time_alloc)
    h=project_feasible(h_start,n)
    p,q=recompute_pq(h); Sp,Sq=build_slopes(h)
    c0=compute_c5(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c0
    # initial all/random
    if n <= 350:
        all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, min(stop-5, t0+time_alloc*0.6?) , DELTA_LARGE maybe, ...)
    else:
        random_pair_search(...)
    # evaluate
    c=compute_c5(h)
    if c < best_c: best...
    # further small/full if remaining
    if time.time() < stop - 20:
       # recompute from best_h? Use current h local
       h=project_feasible(best_h,n)? But current h is local improved.
       # If improved, run another smaller pass for best local

       update best

    elif time.time()... random small
    c=compute_c5(best_h projected)
    return best_h, float(c), n
```

Let's refine `project_feasible` to be fast and exact enough. It is called many times. Iterative 500 loops for each call could be heavy if called many. We call for each candidate and perturb. Optimize:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    s=h.sum()
    diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: return h
    # Use water-fill via binary search? Simpler iterative 100.
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
       # don't clip each loop? need to avoid overshoot: The distribution proportional to room ensures if diff <= tot, h+=diff/tot*room <= h+room=1 since diff/tot<=1; if diff negative similarly. So no clip needed except tiny.
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # small correction
       idx=np.flatnonzero((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))
       if idx.size>0:
          h[idx]+=diff/idx.size
       else:
          h+=diff/n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final adjust? clipping may change sum by tiny; if diff after clip:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       idx=np.flatnonzero((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))
       if idx.size: h[idx]+=diff/idx.size
    return h
```
Since proportional to room avoids overshoot, okay. For diff>0, diff/tot<=1 if diff<=tot (target≤n and sum≤n, yes). For diff<0, |diff|/tot<=1. So no clip each iteration. Good.

Potential if n odd target=125.5, sum can be .5. fine.

Let's write `optimize_candidate` more concretely:
```
def optimize_candidate(h_start, n, alloc, deadline, rng):
    t0=time.time()
    stop = min(deadline, t0 + alloc)
    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    p,q = recompute_pq(h); Sp,Sq=build_slopes(h)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    if time.time() < stop - 10:
        if n <= 300:
            all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, min(stop-5, t0+alloc*0.55), DELTA_LARGE, rng)
        else:
            random_pair_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, min(stop-5, t0+alloc*0.55), DELTA_MEDIUM, rng)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    # second phase with better h and medium deltas if time
    remaining = stop - time.time()
    if remaining > 20:
       h = project_feasible(best_h,n); p,q=...; Sp,Sq=...
       phase_deadline = min(stop-5, time.time() + min(120, remaining*0.4))
       if n <= 300:
          all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, phase_deadline, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng)
       else:
          random_pair_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, phase_deadline, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng)
       c=compute_c5(h); update
    # perturbation attempts while time
    scales = [0.02, 0.05, 0.01, 0.08]
    ks=0
    while time.time() < stop - 15 and ks < 6:
       scale = scales[ks % len(scales)]
       hp = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
       hp = project_feasible(hp, n)
       # optionally if c(hp) > best_c*1.5 maybe skip? But can still optimize; maybe skip to save.
       cp = compute_c5(hp)
       if cp < best_c + 0.02: # reasonable
           p,q=...; Sp,Sq=...
           sub_stop = min(stop-5, time.time() + min(40, max(5, (stop-time.time())*0.3)))
           if n <= 300:
               all_pairs_search(hp,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, sub_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng)
           else:
               random_pair_search(hp,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, sub_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng)
           c=compute_c5(hp)
           if c < best_c: best_h=hp.copy(); best_c=c
       ks +=1
    # final small polish on best if time
    if time.time() < stop - 5:
       h = project_feasible(best_h,n); p,q=...; Sp,Sq=...
       if n <= 300:
          all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, min(stop-2, time.time()+min(60, stop-time.time())), DELTA_SMALL, rng)
       else:
          random_pair_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n, min(stop-2, time.time()+min(30,...)), DELTA_SMALL, rng)
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c5(h); if c<best_c: update
    # final ensure
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), n
```
`alloc*0.55` if alloc large; maybe first phase too long. If `all_pairs_search` stops early due no improvement, returns before. Good.
For n<=300, first phase full list maybe 3 passes due no improvement; if time 400s, it may do many passes? It loops until no improvement or deadline-0.55*alloc. If no improvement after one pass, returns. If improvements each pass, maybe multiple; okay.
For random, pass until deadline.

Need be cautious: `t0+alloc*0.55` could exceed stop; min.
In perturbation, `compute_c5(hp)` before optimization O(n^2), fine. If cp much higher (e.g. 0.5) skip to save. But a high perturbation could lead to new lower basin? All_pairs from high may not reach below initial local within small time. Skip if cp > best_c + 0.05 maybe. Use 0.03? Since target small. We'll skip if cp > best_c + 0.05 to save.

Candidate top optimization may already use 90% time; perturb within each candidate. Then final global polish.

Could we improve by using `all_pairs_search` starting from a random Fourier even if cp high? Maybe if parent local bad. Time budget; try one if time after candidates. In final phase maybe:

   h=random_fourier_h(best_n, seed)
   c=compute; if c < best_c+0.1: optimize for 30s
```

Now, active recompute in all_pairs: We recompute p/q/slopes inside inner loop every 25 improvements. This changes `act_p_nonzero` and `rowpi` references. We also need active_toclear? Let's code carefully.

Pseudo all_pairs_search refined:
```
def all_pairs_search(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,n,deadline,deltas,rng=None):
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline: return ...
    p1=p[1:]; q1=q[1:]
    while time.time() < deadline:
        om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-7*om)
        act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p,q,om,act_tol)
        act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
        act_q_nz = act_q
        slope_tol = 1e-8
        improved = 0
        recompute_count = 0
        for i in range(n-1):
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            oldi = h[i]
            rowpi = Sp[i]
            rowqi = Sq[i]
            for j in range(i+1,n):
                if time.time() > deadline: break
                oldj = h[j]
                allow_pos = False; allow_neg = False
                if p0_active:
                    if oldi > oldj + 1e-10: allow_pos = True
                    elif oldj > oldi + 1e-10: allow_neg = True
                    else: allow_pos = True; allow_neg = True
                if act_p_nz or act_q_nz:
                    rowpj = Sp[j]
                    rowqj = Sq[j]
                    smin = 1e100; smax = -1e100
                    for s in act_p_nz:
                        g = rowpi[s] - rowpj[s]
                        if g < smin: smin = g
                        if g > smax: smax = g
                    for s in act_q_nz:
                        g = rowqi[s] - rowqj[s]
                        if g < smin: smin = g
                        if g > smax: smax = g
                    if smax < -slope_tol: allow_pos = True
                    if smin > slope_tol: allow_neg = True
                if not (allow_pos or allow_neg):
                    continue
                dpos = min(1.0 - oldi, oldj)
                dneg = min(oldi, 1.0 - oldj)
                if dpos <= 0.0 and dneg <= 0.0:
                    continue
                sdist = j - i
                dp1 = rowpi[1:] - rowpj[1:]
                dq1 = rowqi[1:] - rowqj[1:]
                base0 = p[0]
                best_d = 0.0
                best_val = om
                if allow_pos and dpos > 0.0:
                    for d in deltas:
                        if d > dpos + 1e-12:
                            continue
                        val = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, d, sdist)
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = val; best_d = d
                if allow_neg and dneg > 0.0:
                    for d in deltas:
                        if d > dneg + 1e-12:
                            continue
                        val = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, -d, sdist)
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = val; best_d = -d
                if best_d != 0.0 and best_val < om - max(1e-12, 1e-10*om):
                    apply_pair_update(h,p,q,Sp,Sq,i,j,best_d,sdist,dp1,dq1)
                    om = best_val
                    improved += 1
                    recompute_count += 1
                    oldi = h[i]
                    # rowpi,rowqi views updated? They are views into Sp; update_slopes modifies entries, so okay. But if recompute, update.
                    if recompute_count >= 25:
                        p,q = recompute_pq(h)
                        Sp,Sq = build_slopes(h)
                        p1 = p[1:]; q1 = q[1:]
                        om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
                        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-7*om)
                        act_p,act_q,p0_active = active_data(p,q,om,act_tol)
                        act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
                        act_q_nz = act_q
                        rowpi = Sp[i]; rowqi = Sq[i]
                        recompute_count = 0
        if improved == 0:
            break
        # end pass recompute
        p,q = recompute_pq(h)
        Sp,Sq = build_slopes(h)
    return h,p,q,Sp,Sq
```

```
def active_data(p,q,om,tol):
    act_p=[]; act_q=[]
    if p[0] >= om - tol: act_p.append(0)
    for s in range(1,p.size):
       if p[s] >= om - tol: act_p.append(s)
    for s in range(1,q.size):
       if q[s] >= om - tol: act_q.append(s)
    p0 = p[0] >= om - tol
    return act_p, act_q, p0
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np

DELTA_LARGE = (
    0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001,
    0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 0.00005, 0.00002
)
DELTA_MEDIUM = (
    0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001,
    0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 0.00005
)
DELTA_SMALL = (
    0.0001, 0.00005, 0.00002, 0.00001, 0.000005
)


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
        if idx.size:
            h[idx] += diff / float(idx.size)
        else:
            h += diff / float(n)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        idx = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
        if idx.size:
            h[idx] += diff / float(idx.size)
    return h


def resample_h(old_h, new_n):
    old_n = len(old_h)
    if old_n == int(new_n):
        return project_feasible(old_h, int(new_n))

    x_old = (np.arange(old_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(old_n))
    x_new = (np.arange(new_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(new_n))
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old_h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, int(new_n))


def random_fourier_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.full(n, 0.5)

    for k in range(1, 6):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45) * (0.55 ** k)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h += amp * np.cos(np.pi * float(k) * x + ph)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def recompute_pq(h):
    n = len(h)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    p = c[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
    q = c[:n][::-1].copy()
    p0 = float(np.dot(h, 1.0 - h))
    p[0] = p0
    q[0] = p0
    return p, q


def build_slopes(h):
    n = len(h)
    Sp = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=float)
    Sq = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=float)
    idx = np.arange(n)
    Sp[idx, 0] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    Sq[idx, 0] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h

    for s in range(1, n):
        r = n - s
        Sp[:r, s] -= h[s:]
        Sq[:r, s] += 1.0 - h[s:]
        Sp[s:, s] += 1.0 - h[:r]
        Sq[s:, s] -= h[:r]

    return Sp, Sq


def update_slopes(Sp, Sq, k, d):
    if d == 0.0:
        return
    n = Sp.shape[0]
    Sp[k, 0] -= 2.0 * d
    Sq[k, 0] -= 2.0 * d

    lf = k
    if lf > 0:
        s_idx = np.arange(1, lf + 1)
        rows = k - s_idx
        Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d
        Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d

    rt = n - k - 1
    if rt > 0:
        s_idx = np.arange(1, rt + 1)
        rows = k + s_idx
        Sp[rows, s_idx] -= d
        Sq[rows, s_idx] -= d


def active_data(p, q, om, tol):
    act_p = []
    act_q = []
    if p[0] >= om - tol:
        act_p.append(0)
    for s in range(1, len(p)):
        if p[s] >= om - tol:
            act_p.append(s)
    for s in range(1, len(q)):
        if q[s] >= om - tol:
            act_q.append(s)
    p0_active = bool(p[0] >= om - tol)
    return act_p, act_q, p0_active


def pair_eval(p0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, d, sdist):
    newi = oldi + d
    newj = oldj - d

    if newi < -1e-14 or newi > 1.0 + 1e-14 or newj < -1e-14 or newj > 1.0 + 1e-14:
        return float("inf")

    if newi < 0.0:
        newi = 0.0
    elif newi > 1.0:
        newi = 1.0
    if newj < 0.0:
        newj = 0.0
    elif newj > 1.0:
        newj = 1.0

    val = (
        p0
        + newi * (1.0 - newi) - oldi * (1.0 - oldi)
        + newj * (1.0 - newj) - oldj * (1.0 - oldj)
    )

    if p1.size:
        vp = p1 + dp1 * d
        vv = float(vp.max())
        if sdist > 0 and sdist <= p1.size:
            vv2 = float(vp[sdist - 1] + d * d)
            if vv2 > vv:
                vv = vv2
        if vv > val:
            val = vv

        vq = q1 + dq1 * d
        vv = float(vq.max())
        if sdist > 0 and sdist <= q1.size:
            vv2 = float(vq[sdist - 1] + d * d)
            if vv2 > vv:
                vv = vv2
        if vv > val:
            val = vv

    return float(val)


def apply_pair_update(h, p, q, Sp, Sq, i, j, d, sdist, dp1, dq1):
    if d == 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)
    oldi = h[i]
    oldj = h[j]

    newi = oldi + d
    newj = oldj - d
    if newi < 0.0:
        newi = 0.0
    elif newi > 1.0:
        newi = 1.0
    if newj < 0.0:
        newj = 0.0
    elif newj > 1.0:
        newj = 1.0

    p[0] = (
        p[0]
        + newi * (1.0 - newi) - oldi * (1.0 - oldi)
        + newj * (1.0 - newj) - oldj * (1.0 - oldj)
    )

    if n > 1:
        p[1:] += dp1 * d
        q[1:] += dq1 * d
        if sdist > 0:
            p[sdist] += d * d
            q[sdist] += d * d

    h[i] = newi
    h[j] = newj
    update_slopes(Sp, Sq, i, d)
    update_slopes(Sp, Sq, j, -d)


def all_pairs_search(h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, deadline, deltas, rng=None):
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline:
        return h, p, q, Sp, Sq

    p1 = p[1:]
    q1 = q[1:]

    while time.time() < deadline:
        om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-8 * om)
        act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p, q, om, act_tol)
        act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
        act_q_nz = act_q
        slope_tol = 1e-8
        act_small = 1e-10
        improved = 0
        recompute_count = 0

        for i in range(n - 1):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break

            oldi = h[i]
            rowpi = Sp[i]
            rowqi = Sq[i]

            for j in range(i + 1, n):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break

                oldj = h[j]
                rowpj = Sp[j]
                rowqj = Sq[j]

                allow_pos = False
                allow_neg = False

                if p0_active:
                    if oldi > oldj + 1e-10:
                        allow_pos = True
                    elif oldj > oldi + 1e-10:
                        allow_neg = True
                    else:
                        allow_pos = True
                        allow_neg = True

                if act_p_nz or act_q_nz:
                    smin = 1e100
                    smax = -1e100
                    for s in act_p_nz:
                        g = rowpi[s] - rowpj[s]
                        if g < smin:
                            smin = g
                        if g > smax:
                            smax = g
                    for s in act_q_nz:
                        g = rowqi[s] - rowqj[s]
                        if g < smin:
                            smin = g
                        if g > smax:
                            smax = g

                    if smax < -slope_tol:
                        allow_pos = True
                    if smin > slope_tol:
                        allow_neg = True

                if not (allow_pos or allow_neg):
                    continue

                dpos = min(1.0 - oldi, oldj)
                dneg = min(oldi, 1.0 - oldj)
                if dpos <= 0.0 and dneg <= 0.0:
                    continue

                sdist = j - i
                dp1 = rowpi[1:] - rowpj[1:]
                dq1 = rowqi[1:] - rowqj[1:]
                base0 = p[0]

                best_d = 0.0
                best_val = om
                improve_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-10 * om)

                if allow_pos and dpos > 0.0:
                    for dd in deltas:
                        if dd > dpos + act_small:
                            continue
                        vv = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, dd, sdist)
                        if vv < best_val:
                            best_val = vv
                            best_d = dd

                if allow_neg and dneg > 0.0:
                    for dd in deltas:
                        if dd > dneg + act_small:
                            continue
                        vv = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, -dd, sdist)
                        if vv < best_val:
                            best_val = vv
                            best_d = -dd

                if best_d != 0.0 and best_val < om - improve_tol:
                    apply_pair_update(h, p, q, Sp, Sq, i, j, best_d, sdist, dp1, dq1)
                    om = best_val
                    improved += 1
                    recompute_count += 1
                    oldi = h[i]

                    if recompute_count >= 25:
                        p, q = recompute_pq(h)
                        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)
                        p1 = p[1:]
                        q1 = q[1:]
                        om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
                        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-8 * om)
                        act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p, q, om, act_tol)
                        act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
                        act_q_nz = act_q
                        rowpi = Sp[i]
                        rowqi = Sq[i]
                        recompute_count = 0

        if improved == 0:
            break

        if time.time() > deadline - 2:
            break

        p, q = recompute_pq(h)
        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)

    return h, p, q, Sp, Sq


def random_pair_search(h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, deadline, deltas, rng):
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline:
        return h, p, q, Sp, Sq

    p1 = p[1:]
    q1 = q[1:]

    om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
    act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-8 * om)
    act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p, q, om, act_tol)
    act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
    act_q_nz = act_q
    slope_tol = 1e-8
    act_small = 1e-10

    attempts = 0
    skips = 0
    no_imp = 0
    recompute_count = 0
    max_attempts = 300000

    while time.time() < deadline and attempts < max_attempts:
        i = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
        j = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
        if i == j:
            continue

        oldi = h[i]
        oldj = h[j]
        rowpi = Sp[i]
        rowqi = Sq[i]
        rowpj = Sp[j]
        rowqj = Sq[j]

        allow_pos = False
        allow_neg = False

        if p0_active:
            if oldi > oldj + 1e-10:
                allow_pos = True
            elif oldj > oldi + 1e-10:
                allow_neg = True
            else:
                allow_pos = True
                allow_neg = True

        if act_p_nz or act_q_nz:
            smin = 1e100
            smax = -1e100
            for s in act_p_nz:
                g = rowpi[s] - rowpj[s]
                if g < smin:
                    smin = g
                if g > smax:
                    smax = g
            for s in act_q_nz:
                g = rowqi[s] - rowqj[s]
                if g < smin:
                    smin = g
                if g > smax:
                    smax = g

            if smax < -slope_tol:
                allow_pos = True
            if smin > slope_tol:
                allow_neg = True

        if not (allow_pos or allow_neg):
            skips += 1
            if skips > 40000:
                break
            attempts += 1
            continue

        skips = 0
        dpos = min(1.0 - oldi, oldj)
        dneg = min(oldi, 1.0 - oldj)
        if dpos <= 0.0 and dneg <= 0.0:
            attempts += 1
            continue

        sdist = abs(i - j)
        dp1 = rowpi[1:] - rowpj[1:]
        dq1 = rowqi[1:] - rowqj[1:]
        base0 = p[0]
        best_d = 0.0
        best_val = om
        improve_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-10 * om)

        if allow_pos and dpos > 0.0:
            for dd in deltas:
                if dd > dpos + act_small:
                    continue
                vv = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, dd, sdist)
                if vv < best_val:
                    best_val = vv
                    best_d = dd

        if allow_neg and dneg > 0.0:
            for dd in deltas:
                if dd > dneg + act_small:
                    continue
                vv = pair_eval(base0, p1, q1, oldi, oldj, dp1, dq1, -dd, sdist)
                if vv < best_val:
                    best_val = vv
                    best_d = -dd

        if best_d != 0.0 and best_val < om - improve_tol:
            apply_pair_update(h, p, q, Sp, Sq, i, j, best_d, sdist, dp1, dq1)
            om = best_val
            no_imp = 0
            recompute_count += 1

            if recompute_count >= 100:
                p, q = recompute_pq(h)
                Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)
                p1 = p[1:]
                q1 = q[1:]
                om = max(float(np.max(p)), float(np.max(q)))
                act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-8 * om)
                act_p, act_q, p0_active = active_data(p, q, om, act_tol)
                act_p_nz = [s for s in act_p if s != 0]
                act_q_nz = act_q
                recompute_count = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > 30000:
                break

        attempts += 1

    return h, p, q, Sp, Sq


def candidate_sort_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_candidate(cands, seen, c, n, h, min_n=32, max_n=600):
    n = int(n)
    if not (min_n <= n <= max_n):
        return
    if n in seen:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(c):
        return
    seen.add(n)
    cands.append((float(c), n, h))


def optimize_candidate(h_start, n, alloc, deadline, rng):
    t0 = time.time()
    stop = min(float(deadline), t0 + float(alloc))
    n = int(n)

    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    p, q = recompute_pq(h)
    Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    # Phase 1: main local pair descent.
    if time.time() < stop - 10:
        phase_stop = min(stop - 5.0, t0 + max(20.0, 0.55 * float(alloc)))
        if n <= 300:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = all_pairs_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, phase_stop, DELTA_LARGE, rng
            )
        else:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = random_pair_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, phase_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )

        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = float(c)

    # Phase 2: repeat with medium steps on the best point.
    remaining = float(stop - time.time())
    if remaining > 20.0:
        h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
        p, q = recompute_pq(h)
        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)
        phase_stop = min(stop - 5.0, time.time() + min(90.0, 0.35 * remaining))
        if n <= 300:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = all_pairs_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, phase_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )
        else:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = random_pair_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, phase_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )
        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = float(c)

    # Phase 3: short multi-restarts around the best point.
    scales = (0.01, 0.02, 0.04, 0.06, 0.015, 0.03)
    for kk in range(len(scales)):
        if time.time() > stop - 12.0:
            break
        scale = scales[kk]
        hp = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
        hp = project_feasible(hp, n)
        cp = compute_c5(hp)
        if cp > best_c + 0.05:
            continue

        p, q = recompute_pq(hp)
        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(hp)
        sub_stop = min(
            stop - 5.0,
            time.time() + min(45.0, max(10.0, 0.30 * float(stop - time.time())))
        )
        if n <= 300:
            hp, p, q, Sp, Sq = all_pairs_search(
                hp, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, sub_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )
        else:
            hp, p, q, Sp, Sq = random_pair_search(
                hp, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, sub_stop, DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )

        c = compute_c5(hp)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = hp.copy()
            best_c = float(c)

    # Phase 4: final small-step polish.
    if time.time() < stop - 4.0:
        h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
        p, q = recompute_pq(h)
        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)
        if n <= 300:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = all_pairs_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, min(stop - 1.0, time.time() + 45.0), DELTA_SMALL, rng
            )
        else:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = random_pair_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, n, min(stop - 1.0, time.time() + 25.0), DELTA_SMALL, rng
            )
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = float(c)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 6.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1.0)
        if par.size > 0:
            parent = par
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    cands = []
    seen = set()

    if parent is not None:
        npar = int(len(parent))
        par = project_feasible(parent, npar)
        cpar = compute_c5(par)
        add_candidate(cands, seen, cpar, npar, par.copy())

        base_n = npar
        ratios = (1.0, 0.8, 0.65, 0.5, 0.4, 1.2, 1.35, 1.55, 1.8, 2.2)
        for r in ratios:
            nn = int(round(float(base_n) * float(r)))
            if nn in seen:
                continue
            hh = resample_h(par, nn)
            cc = compute_c5(hh)
            add_candidate(cands, seen, cc, nn, hh)

        fixed_ns = (64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 320, 384, 480, 576)
        for nn in fixed_ns:
            if nn in seen:
                continue
            hh = resample_h(par, nn)
            cc = compute_c5(hh)
            add_candidate(cands, seen, cc, nn, hh)

    else:
        base_n = 250
        add_candidate(cands, seen, compute_c5(random_fourier_h(base_n, int(seed) + 1)),
                      base_n, random_fourier_h(base_n, int(seed) + 1))
        add_candidate(cands, seen, compute_c5(random_fourier_h(base_n, int(seed) + 2)),
                      base_n, random_fourier_h(base_n, int(seed) + 2))
        add_candidate(cands, seen, compute_c5(random_fourier_h(192, int(seed) + 3)),
                      192, random_fourier_h(192, int(seed) + 3))
        add_candidate(cands, seen, compute_c5(random_fourier_h(320, int(seed) + 4)),
                      320, random_fourier_h(320, int(seed) + 4))

    if not cands:
        n = 250
        h = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
        return h, compute_c5(h), n

    cands.sort(key=candidate_sort_key)
    top = cands[:5]

    best_h = top[0][2].copy()
    best_c = float(top[0][0])

    total_budget = max(60.0, float(deadline - time.time()) - 30.0)
    if len(top) == 1:
        fracs = (0.95,)
    elif len(top) == 2:
        fracs = (0.65, 0.28)
    elif len(top) == 3:
        fracs = (0.55, 0.27, 0.14)
    elif len(top) == 4:
        fracs = (0.48, 0.25, 0.15, 0.09)
    else:
        fracs = (0.42, 0.23, 0.15, 0.10, 0.07)

    for idx in range(len(top)):
        if time.time() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        c_start, n_start, h_start = top[idx]
        if c_start > best_c + 0.05 and best_c < 0.381:
            continue
        alloc = total_budget * float(fracs[idx])
        if alloc < 25.0:
            continue
        h_opt, c_opt, n_opt = optimize_candidate(
            h_start, n_start, alloc, deadline, rng
        )
        if c_opt < best_c:
            best_c = float(c_opt)
            best_h = h_opt.copy()

    # Final global polish on the best returned candidate.
    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        nb = int(len(best_h))
        h = project_feasible(best_h, nb)
        p, q = recompute_pq(h)
        Sp, Sq = build_slopes(h)
        if nb <= 300:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = all_pairs_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, nb,
                min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + 45.0),
                DELTA_SMALL, rng
            )
        else:
            h, p, q, Sp, Sq = random_pair_search(
                h, p, q, Sp, Sq, nb,
                min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + 25.0),
                DELTA_MEDIUM, rng
            )
        h = project_feasible(h, nb)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = float(c)
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
